'/*main*/
Sub Makro1()
	Dim Uid
	Dim Password, Basla, objxmlDoc, URL, URL2, k,z,Ek,dosya	

'	Set OTOSP1E=Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E")
'	Set OTOSP1T=Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1T")	
	ReDim GecersizKodlar(0)
	Webden = 0
	if Webden = 1 then
		Uid=Trim(OTOSP1E.UID)
		Password=Trim(OTOSP1E.PASSWORD)
		if Uid="" or Password="" then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Kullanýcý Adý veya Þifre Boþ Olamaz. Ýþlem Ýptal.","dinamo",MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if
	basla = Timer
	OTOSP1T.Empty
	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False
	URL = "https://fosn.ford.com.tr/prg_daily_summary_xml.asp?e=1"
	URL2 = "https://fosn.ford.com.tr/prg_weekly_summary_xml.asp?e=1"
	if Webden = 1 then
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ford.com.tr 'ye baðlanýlýyor"
		k = Doc.HTTPRequest("<https://login.ford.com.tr/authenticate.asp?userid=" & Uid & "&password=" & Password)
		if trim(k) = "" then
			z = Doc.D7MsgBox("ford.com.tr 'ye baðlanýlamýyor." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
			Doc.StatusBarMessage ""
			exit Sub
		end if
		Ek = "<?xml version=" & chr(34) & "1.0" & chr(34) & " encoding=" & chr(34) & "ISO-8859-9" & chr(34) &"?>"
		objXMLDoc.Load URL
	else
		dosya = "c:\FO_GUNLUKP.xml"
		objXMLDoc.Load dosya
	end if
	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Günlük program alýnýyor"

	if trim(objXMLDoc.xml) = "" then
		z = Doc.D7MsgBox("Günlük program alýnamadý." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage ""
		exit Sub
	end if
	objXMLDoc.LoadXML Ek & vbcrlf & objXMLDoc.xml
	
'	msgbox objXMLDoc.xml
	Dim i,y, Root,NodeList, ST40, kk, Attr, Schedule, Qty, TesTarih, SatirNo, yy, Sc

	y = 1
	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	Set NodeList = Root.getElementsByTagName("PART")

	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor"

	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	
	kk = ElemanSayisi(objXMLDoc)
	For i = 0 To NodeList.Length - 1
		Set Attr = NodeList.Item(i).Attributes
		Set Schedule = NodeList.Item(i).getElementsByTagName("SCHEDULE_ITEM")
		For Each Sc In Schedule
			Set Qty = Sc.SelectSingleNode("NET_QUANTITY")
			Set TesTarih = Sc.SelectSingleNode("DATE")
			If Qty.Text <> "0" Then 
				If y <> 1 Then
					k = ST40.AddRow()
				Else
					If ST40.GetRecCount = 0 Then
						k = ST40.AddRow()
					End If
				End If
				y = 2
				SatirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
				ST40.KOD(satirNo) = Temizle(Attr.Item(0).Text)
				ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Qty.Text
				ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = FmtTarih(TesTarih.Text)
				yy = yy + 1 
				Doc.StatusBarMessage "XML okunuyor : " & yy & " / " & kk
			End If
		Next
	Next

	Dim Btarih, STarih,Fark,Ptesi, stSatirSayisi, sonKolon
	BTarih = IlkTarih(ST40)

	Starih = Sontarih(ST40)
	Fark = 8 - Weekday(Starih, 2)
	Ptesi = replace(Doc.StrDate_Increment(Starih, Fark),"/","")

	ST40.Sort("KOD+RTESTARIH")
	ST40.GroupBy("KOD+RTESTARIH")


	Dim Tarih, rep,sKol, Miktar, res, KodVar, StokKodu,MstokKodu, Birim
	stSatirSayisi = ST40.GetRecCount()
	SonKolon = 1
	For i = 1 To stSatirSayisi
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & stSatirSayisi
		StokKodu = ST40.KOD(i)
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,"")
		if trim(Birim) = "" then
			MStokKodu = Doc.Get0G0U(StokKodu,OTOSP1E.CH_KODU,"12")
			if trim(MStokKodu) = "" or trim(MStokKodu) = trim(StokKodu) then
				KodVar = 0
			else
				KodVar = 1
			end if
		else
			KodVar = 1
		end if
		if KodVar = 1 then
			If OTOSP1T.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				OTOSP1T.AddRow
				satirNo = OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
				OTOSP1T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				OTOSP1T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
			ElseIf OTOSP1T.GetRecCount = 1 And trim(OTOSP1T.KOD(1)) = "" Then
				satirNo = 1
				OTOSP1T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
			Else
				satirNo = satirBul(ST40.KOD(i))
				if satirNo = 0 then
					OTOSP1T.AddRow
					satirNo = OTOSP1T.GetRecCount()
				end if
				OTOSP1T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				OTOSP1T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
			End If
			Tarih = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
			Rep = AlanBul(Tarih,BTarih)
			sKol = cint(Replace(Rep,"REPCOL",""))
			if sKol > SonKolon then SonKolon = SKol
			if Tarih > STarih then STarih = Tarih
			Miktar = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T",Rep,satirNo,Miktar + OTOSP1T.GetFieldvalue(Rep,satirNo))
		else
			if GecersizBul(StokKodu) = 0 then
				GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)) = StokKodu
				Redim Preserve GecersizKodlar(uBound(GecersizKodlar)+1)
			end if
		end if
	Next
	OTOSP1E.ACIKLAMA = "Baþlangýç tarihi : " & Doc.StrToNordate(BTarih)

	dim bitis, sCol, j, BaslangicKolon

	bitis = Timer
	Set ST40 = Nothing
	Set ObjXMLDoc = Nothing

	Fark = 8 - Weekday(Starih, 2)
	Ptesi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Starih, Fark)
	sCol = CInt(replace(SonKolon,"REPCOL",""))

	for j = 1 to OTOSP1T.GetRecCount
		For i = 1 to sCol
			Tarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BTarih,i-1)	
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","TARIH" & i,j,Tarih)	
			res = Doc.SetFieldValue("OTOSP1T","ATTRIB" & i,j,"G")	
		Next
	Next
	BaslangicKolon = SonKolon + 1
	Call HaftalikPlan(Ptesi, BaslangicKolon)
	Call Tamamla()
	Call ToplamHesapla()

	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_SETCAPTIONS")
	Doc.RunCommand("CMD_STOK_VE_MPS_HESAPLA")
	if Webden = 1 then
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "ford.com.tr baðlantýsý kesiliyor"
		k = Doc.HTTPRequest("<https://fosn.ford.com.tr/exit.asp")
	end if
	dim ret, Gecersiz, Gecersizler
	for each Gecersiz in GecersizKodlar
		Gecersizler = Gecersizler & Gecersiz & vbcrlf
	Next
	if trim(replace(Gecersizler,vbcrlf,"")) <> "" then
		ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("Geçersiz Kodlar : " & vbcrlf &	Gecersizler,"dinamo",MB_OK)
	end if
	msgbox "Bitti " & bitis - basla & " s"
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TNT2
Hangi Tablo için : TNT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TNT yükleme formunu sipariþine aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(TNT00E.TESLIM) = "" or trim(TNT00E.CH_KODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve teslim yerini girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	For i = 1 to TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "TNT Yükleme Formu Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		if TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i) > 0 Then
			ST40.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = TNT00T.KOD(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = TNT00E.TESLIM
		end if
	Next
	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")	
	ST40.Sort("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("TNT00E", TNT00E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO",0)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
	EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
	ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	ST40E.CHSIPNO = TNT00E.EVRAKNO
	ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""

	ST40T.Empty

	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Satýþ sipariþine aktarýlýyor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	Msgbox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý"
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
 Dim T_TNT00E    
 Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")   
 Dim nret    
 nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)   
 Dim T_U1    
 Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
 K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
 T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FORDMUT
Hangi Tablo için : CARI00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ford Mutabakat Text Dosyasý
Kod : 
Public FirmaKodu, KayitSayisi
Sub Makro1()
	Const ForReading = 1, ForWriting = 2, ForAppending = 8
	KayitSayisi = 0

	FirmaKodu = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!FORDCH"))
	if trim(FirmaKodu) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("!FORDCH parametresini yaratarak Text alanýna Firmanýzýn Ford 'daki C/H kodunu girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf _
									& vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir.")
		exit Sub
	end if

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Klasor = "c:\Ford Mutabakat"

	if not fso.FolderExists(Klasor) then
		k = Doc.d7Msgbox(Klasor & " klasörü bulunamadý yaratmak ister misiniz?","dinamo",MB_YESNO)
		if k = 6 then
			Set f = fso.CreateFolder(Klasor)
			if f is nothing then
				k = Doc.d7Msgbox("Klasör yaratýlamadý" & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
				exit sub		
			end if
		else
			k = Doc.d7Msgbox("Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
			exit sub		
		end if
	end if

	DosyaTarihi = Replace(Doc.BuGun,"/","")
	BitisTarihi = Replace(TL_EKSTRE.BITISTARIHI,"/","")
	if trim(BitisTarihi) = "" or trim(TL_EKSTRE.DEVIRTARIHI) = "" then
		k = Doc.d7Msgbox("Ekstre için baþlangýç ve bitiþ tarihi girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub		
	end if
	dosyaAdi = Klasor & "\" & FirmaKodu & DosyaTarihi & ".txt"

	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi, ForWriting, True)

	TransferBaslangic = "RCLHDR" & Sol(FirmaKodu,10) & DosyaTarihi & BitisTarihi
	TFile.WriteLine TransferBaslangic
	KayitSayisi = 	KayitSayisi + 1
	For i=1 To DT03.GetRecCount
		If trim(DT03.EVRAKTYPE(i)) <> "DV" Then
			EvrakTarihi = Replace(DT03.TARIH(i),"/","")
			EvrakNo = sag(DT03.EVRAKNO(i),10)
			Aciklama = sol(DT03.ACIKLAMA(i),50)
			if trim(Aciklama) = "" then Aciklama = sol(DT03.EVRAKTYPE(i) & "-" & trim(DT03.EVRAKNO(i)),50)
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, "-"," ")
			Borc = Ondalikli(DT03.BORCFIYAT(i),17,2)
			Alacak = Ondalikli(DT03.ALACAKFIYAT(i),17,2)
			Bakiye = Ondalikli(DT03.BORCBAKIYESI(i) - DT03.ALACAKBAKIYESI(i),18,2)
	
			EkstreDetayi = "RCLLST" & EvrakTarihi & EvrakNo & Aciklama & Borc & Alacak & Bakiye                      
			TFile.WriteLine (EkstreDetayi)
			KayitSayisi = 	KayitSayisi + 1
		End If
	Next
	KayitSayisi = 	KayitSayisi + 1
	TransferBitis = "RCLTRL" & Sag(KayitSayisi,6)
	TFile.WriteLine TransferBitis
	TFile.Close
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(1,"h",-1,-1,"")	
	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.SetMailSubject("Mutabakat - " & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")))
	res = pe1.AddMailReceiver("mutabakat@ford.com.tr",1)
	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(DosyaAdi,0)
	res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)
	res = pe1.Maildoc()
	res = pe1.Close()
End Sub
Function Sol(Yazi, KarakterSayisi)
	Sol = Left(Trim(Yazi) & String(KarakterSayisi, " "), KarakterSayisi)
End Function
Function Sag(Yazi, KarakterSayisi)
	Sag = Right(String(KarakterSayisi, "0") & Trim(Yazi), KarakterSayisi)
End Function
Function SagSpace(Yazi, KarakterSayisi)
	SagSpace = Right(String(KarakterSayisi, " ") & Trim(Yazi), KarakterSayisi)
End Function
Function Ondalikli(Sayi, KarakterSayisi, OndalikSayisi)
	Sayi = Doc.NumFormat0(Sayi, KarakterSayisi, OndalikSayisi)
	Sayi = Replace(Sayi, ",","")
	Sayi = Replace(Sayi, ".",",")

	Ondalikli = sagSpace(Sayi,KarakterSayisi)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	STOK19T.SafePack
	if trim(STOK19E.SHIPTOCODE) = "" or trim(STOK19E.CHKODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve sevk adres kodunu girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	For i = 1 to STOK19T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Malzeme listesi okunuyor : " & i & " / " & STOK19T.GetRecCount()
		ST40.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40.KOD(satirNo) = STOK19T.KOD(i)
		ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = STOK19E.TARIH
		ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = STOK19E.TARIH
		ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = STOK19E.SHIPTOCODE
	Next
	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")	
	ST40.Sort("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK19E", STOK19E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO",0)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
	EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= STOK19E.CHKODU
	ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	ST40E.SEVKADRESI_1 = STOK19E.SEVKADRESI_1

	ST40T.Empty

	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		if i = 100 then exit sub
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Satýþ sipariþine aktarýlýyor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	Msgbox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý"
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
	Dim T_STOK19E    
	Set T_STOK19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK19E",T_STOK19E.EVRAKNO)   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : CHEP
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Chep ASN
Kod : 
Dim S60T, S60T1, Seperator, ChepID, SevkiyatRef
Sub Makro1()
	STOK60T.SafePack()

	if HataVarmi() Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Kesildi", "", MB_OK
	End if
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("sdfg234hdfh", "STOK60T_CCC", "NOTES>' '", "NOTES",  -1)

	Seperator = ";"

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Klasor = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT"
	Call YoksaYarat(fso, Klasor)
	EskiId = ""
	DosyaAdi = ""
	siraNo = 0
	SevkiyatRef = CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60E", STOK60E.EVRAKNO, "FATURANO", 0))
	if trim(SevkiyatRef) = "" Then
		ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("KTA 'daki fatura no alaný doldurulmamýþ. Devam etmek istiyor musunuz?", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
		if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
		SevkiyatRef = STOK60E.EVRAKNO
	End if
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		ChepID = trim(S60T.NOTES(i))
		if ChepID <> EskiId Then
			if DosyaAdi <> "" Then
				Call FooterYaz(TFile)
				TFile.Close
				ret = FTPGonder(DosyaAdi)
			End if
			Set S60T1 = Doc.Select2("sdfgwrtjdfj", S60T.GetAppName, "NOTES='" & ChepID & "'", "", -1)
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
			DosyaAdi = Klasor & "\CHP" & trim(SevkiyatRef) & "-" & siraNo & "-" & ChepID & ".txt"
			Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi, 2, True)
			Call HeaderYaz(TFile)
		End if
		EskiId = ChepID
		Call SatirBilgileriniYaz(TFile, i)
	Next
	if DosyaAdi <> "" Then
		Call FooterYaz(TFile)
		TFile.Close
		ret = FTPGonder(DosyaAdi)
	End if
End Sub
Sub HeaderYaz(TFile)
	SequenceNumber = Doc.Cdbl(Doc.Sayac0danAl("FSAYAC"))

	Baslik = "*****+"  												'Begin Header	Fix '*****+'	M	AN	6	'*****+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "FROM+CHEP-"							'FROM	Fix: 'FROM+CHEP-'	M	AN	10	'FROM+CHEP-'
	Baslik = Baslik & "AV"										'Communicator-Country	ISO Country Code -  "AV" for Automotive Customers	M	AN	2		
	Baslik = Baslik & CHEPID									'Communicator-Code	CHEP's 10 digit Global ID	M	AN	10		
	Baslik = Baslik & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "RCVD+"									'RCVD	Qualifier for the file date 	M	AN	5	'RCVD+	
	Baslik = Baslik & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "")	'File Date	File Date - CCYYMMDD	M	AN	8		
	Baslik = Baslik & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "FREF+"									'FREF	Qualifier for file reference	M	AN	5	'FREF+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & SequenceNumber						'Sequence Num	This is a File Sequence Number for the whole file and is used by CHEP to check for duplicate and missing files.  Each new file should be incremented by 1.	M	AN	14		
	Baslik = Baslik & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "NORC+"
	Baslik = Baslik & S60T1.GetRowCount()				'Num Records	Total Number of Records, without Header and Trailer, included in this file	M	N	9		
	Baslik = Baslik & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "SEPR+"									'SEPR	Qualifier for the file separator, fixed text 'SEPR+'	M	AN	5	SEPR+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & Seperator								'Used separator sign	The character used as the field delimiter to separate fields in the detail record.  Since this file is a variable record length, we need a certain character to separate fields in the detail records (such as ,~|\/*+:; and ^).	M	AN	1		
	Baslik = Baslik & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "VERS+"									'VERS	Qualifier for the version of the file, fixed text 'VERS+'	M	AN	5	'VERS+'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "1.02"									'Version of this Layout	Fix: '1.00'	M	AN	4	'1.00'	
	Baslik = Baslik & "+*****"									'End of Line	Fixed text:   '+*****'	M	AN	6	+*****'	

	TFile.WriteLine Baslik
End Sub
Sub SatirBilgileriniYaz(TFile, satirNo)
	ReceiverCode = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SHPT00J", "TELEFONNO_1", STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE, "")

	Satir = "LI="														'Start of Record Delimiter	Char 3	3 characters "LI=" to indicate new record.  	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & satirNo									'Sequence Number	NUM 6 	Detail Record number within transmission.  This starts at 1 for the first record in each transmission, and increases by 1 for each record. 	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & "1"											'Informer Flag	CHAR 1	Used to determine informer location.  If you are the sender of CHEP equipment, set this to 1.  If you are the receiver of CHEP equipment, set this to 2.  1 =  Sender / 2 = Receiver	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & "TR"										'Informer Country Code	CHAR 2	ISO code e.g. DE, ES,UK,FR	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & "SA"										'Sender Code Qualifier 	CHAR 2	Fixed Length. Type of code being used for the Sender.  CHEP uses this to determine the meaning of the code you send in the next field. CHEP Global ID (10 digit) = SA Old CHEP code (6 to 8 digit) = CC  Customers (your) internal number = IN	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & CHEPID										'Sender Code	CHAR35	Code provided by you (the customer) for the sender of the equipment, as indicated above	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & "IN"										'Receiver Code Qualifier 	CHAR 2	Fixed Length. Type of code being used for the Receiver.  CHEP uses this to determine the meaning of the code you send in the next field. CHEP Global ID (10 digit) = SA Old CHEP code (6 to 8 digit) = CC  Customers (your) internal number = IN	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & ReceiverCode							'Receiver Code	CHAR 35 	Code provided by you (the customer) for the receiver of the equipment, as indicated above.	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & "92"										'Equipment Code Qualifier	CHAR 2	You may enter CHEP's old Equipment Code; CHEP's new Equipment Code or your own Equipment Code in the next field.  This field tells CHEP how to translate it.   9 = EAN (International Article Numbering Association) 90 = CHEP Global Equipment Code  91 = CHEP old Equipment code  92 = Customers (your) internal Equipment code	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S60T.KOD(satirNo), STOK60E.CH_KODU))	'Equipment Code	CHAR  35	Code to describe the type of equipment being moved, as indicated above.	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & Replace(STOK60E.TARIH, "/", "")		'Date of Dispatch (DoD)	DATE 8	Fixed Length.  Date of Dispatch of the equipment, in the format : CCYYMMDD If you are the sender of the equipment, please complete this date.  If you are the receiver of the equipment, then leave this blank, and complete the next field.  In some special circumstances, both fields may be completed.	Yes 	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Date of Receipt (DoR)	DATE 8	Fixed Length.  Date of Receipt: CCYYMMDD See notes for previous field.	Yes 	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator
	Satir = Satir & S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)		'Quantity 	NUM 7 	Movement quantity.  Always positive, no sign, no decimals	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & trim(SevkiyatRef)					'Reference 1	CHAR 13	Reference 1 is your main reference for this transaction (Pick up sheets for GM)	Yes	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Reference 2	CHAR 13	Reference 2 is a secondary reference for this transaction	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Reference 3	CHAR 13	Reference 3 will be treated as a batch reference on your invoice transaction listing	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Transport responsibility	CHAR 1	1 = CHEP / 2 = Sender / 3 = Receiver.  Normally this will be set to 2.  	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'System Parameter 1	NUMC 1	CHEP Internal Use	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'System Parameter 2	CHAR 1	CHEP Internal Use	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'System Parameter 3	CHAR 6	CHEP Internal Use	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'System Parameter 4	CHAR3	CHEP Internal Use	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'System Parameter 5	NUM 1	CHEP Internal Use	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
'	Satir = Satir & ""											'The following name and address fields refer to the "other party" in the transaction.  If the informer (you) are the sender, then this is the address of the receiver, and vice versa.  This information is Mandatory if you are using your own codes (Qualifier "IN")	
'	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Counter Part Name	CHAR 40	Business Name	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Counter Part Address	CHAR 60 	Street Address	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Counter Part City	CHAR 40	Town/City	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Counter Part Post Code / Zipcode	CHAR 30	Post Code or Zip code	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Counter Part State/Region	Char 35	Common names or abbreviations for regions or states or provinces or counties.  	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & "TR"										'Counter Part Country	CHAR 2	Must be an ISO code - This field is mandatory only when using qualifiers CC or IN for the Counter Part code.	Conditional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Third Party Code Qualifier 	CHAR 2	Fixed Length. Type of code being used by the Third Party.  CHEP uses this to determine the meaning of the code you send in the next field. CHEP Global ID (10 digit) = SA Customers (your) internal number = IN	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & ""											'Third Party Code	CHAR 35	This code is used where equipment is sent from one location, delivered to another and a third party is to be charged for part or all of the movement.	Optional	
	Satir = Satir & Seperator								
	Satir = Satir & "<"											'End of record marker	Char 1	A single character "<" to indicate the end of the record.		

	TFile.WriteLine Satir
End Sub
Sub FooterYaz(TFile)
	Footer = "*****+"												'Begin Trailer	Indicating a trailer record	M	AN	6	'*****+'	
	Footer = Footer & "NORC+"									'NORC	Qualifier for number of records	O	AN	5	'NORC+'	
	Footer = Footer & S60T1.GetRowCount()				'Num Records	Total Number of Records, without Header and Trailer.  This should be the same as in the header	O	N	9		
	Footer = Footer & "+"											'Separator	'+'	M	AN	1	'+'	
	Footer = Footer & "SQTY+"									'SQTY	Qualifier for Sum of the quantity	O	AN	5	'SQTY+'	
	Footer = Footer & S60T1.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")		'Sum of QTY	Hash Total.  A sum of all Quantities in the file, all products, both directions.  This is used as a secondary check to ensure that the records were received cleanly.  If set to zero the file will not be processed	O	N	12		
	Footer = Footer & "+EOF"									'End Trailer	End of file	M	AN	4	'+EOF'	

	TFile.WriteLine Footer
End Sub
Sub YoksaYarat(fso, Klasor)
	if not fso.FolderExists(Klasor) Then
		fso.CreateFolder Klasor
	End if
End Sub
Function HataVarmi()
	HataVarmi = False
	Set gggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	if trim(STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7msgbox "Sevk Adres kodu seçilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
		HataVarmi = True
	Else
		if trim(CStr(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SHPT00J", "TELEFONNO_1", STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE, ""))) = "" Then
			Doc.d7msgbox trim(STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE) & " Sevk Adres kartý Telefon No alanýna Chep 'te tanýmlý fabrika kodu girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
			HataVarmi = True
		End if
	End if

	Set S60T1 = Doc.Select2G("STOK60T_CCC", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI='E'", "", "KOD", -1)

	For i = 1 to S60T1.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S60T1.KOD(i), STOK60E.CH_KODU)) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(S60T1.KOD(i)) & " müþteri/tedarikçi stok kodu girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
			HataVarmi = True
		End if
		ChepId = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_1", S60T1.KOD(i), "")
		if trim(ChepId) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(S60T1.KOD(i)) & " Stok Kartý Text-1 'de CHEP ID girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
			HataVarmi = False
		End if
		S60T1.NOTES(i) = ChepId
	Next
End Function
Function FTPGonder(DosyAdi)
	ServerName = "ftp.map.com.tr"
	Port = 21
	remote_path = "/Chep/Inbox/"
	kullanici = "dostel"
	sifre = "asdasd"
'	FTPGonder = FtpSend(ServerName, Port, kullanici,sifre,DosyAdi,remote_path)
	FTPGonder = FTPUpload(ServerName, Port, kullanici, sifre, DosyAdi, remote_path)
End Function
Function FtpSend(ServerName, Port, kullanici,sifre,local_path,remote_path)
    Set p=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
    if CInt(Port)>0 then
        p.SetParameter "PORT",CInt(Port)
    end if

'msgbox ServerName & "," & kullanici & "," & sifre & "," & local_path & "," & remote_path
    FtpSend=Doc.FtpPutFile(ServerName,kullanici,sifre,local_path,remote_path,p)
End Function
Function FTPUpload(sSite, sPort, sUsername, sPassword, sLocalFile, sRemotePath)
	Const OpenAsDefault = -2
	Const FailIfNotExist = 0
	Const ForReading = 1
	Const ForWriting = 2
  
	Set oFTPScriptFSO = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Set oFTPScriptShell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")

	sRemotePath = Trim(sRemotePath)
	sLocalFile = Trim(sLocalFile)
  
	If InStr(sRemotePath, " ") > 0 Then
		If Left(sRemotePath, 1) <> """" And Right(sRemotePath, 1) <> """" Then
			sRemotePath = """" & sRemotePath & """"
		End If
	End If
  
	If InStr(sLocalFile, " ") > 0 Then
		If Left(sLocalFile, 1) <> """" And Right(sLocalFile, 1) <> """" Then
			sLocalFile = """" & sLocalFile & """"
		End If
	End If

	If Len(sRemotePath) = 0 Then
		sRemotePath = "\"
	End If
  
	If InStr(sLocalFile, "*") Then
		If InStr(sLocalFile, " ") Then
			FTPUpload = "Error: Wildcard uploads do not work if the path contains a " & _
			"space." & vbCrLf
			FTPUpload = FTPUpload & "This is a limitation of the Microsoft FTP client."
			Exit Function
		End If
	ElseIf Len(sLocalFile) = 0 Or Not oFTPScriptFSO.FileExists(sLocalFile) Then
		FTPUpload = "Error: File Not Found."
		Exit Function
	End If
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "open" & " " & sSite & " " & sPort & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "USER " & sUsername & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & sPassword & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "cd " & sRemotePath & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "binary" & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "prompt n" & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "put " & sLocalFile & vbCrLf
	sFTPScript = sFTPScript & "quit" & vbCrLf & "quit" & vbCrLf & "quit" & vbCrLf


	sFTPTemp = oFTPScriptShell.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%TEMP%")
	sFTPTempFile = sFTPTemp & "\" & oFTPScriptFSO.GetTempName
	sFTPResults = sFTPTemp & "\" & oFTPScriptFSO.GetTempName

	Set fFTPScript = oFTPScriptFSO.CreateTextFile(sFTPTempFile, True)
	fFTPScript.WriteLine (sFTPScript)
	fFTPScript.Close
	Set fFTPScript = Nothing

	oFTPScriptShell.Run "%comspec% /c FTP -n -s:" & sFTPTempFile & " " & " > " & sFTPResults, 0, True
  
	Set fFTPResults = oFTPScriptFSO.OpenTextFile(sFTPResults, ForReading, _
	FailIfNotExist, OpenAsDefault)
	sResults = fFTPResults.ReadAll
	fFTPResults.Close
  
	oFTPScriptFSO.DeleteFile (sFTPTempFile)
	oFTPScriptFSO.DeleteFile (sFTPResults)
  
	If InStr(sResults, "226 Transfer complete.") > 0 or InStr(sResults, "226 File receive OK.") > 0 Then
		FTPUpload = True
		MsgBox "Dosya Gönderildi"
		STOK60E.NOTES_4 = "CHEP ASN GÖNDERÝLDÝ"
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	ElseIf InStr(sResults, "File not found") > 0 Then
		FTPUpload = "Error: File Not Found"
		MsgBox "Hata : " & vbcrlf & sResults
	ElseIf InStr(sResults, "cannot log in.") > 0 Then
		FTPUpload = "Error: Login Failed."
		MsgBox "Hata : " & vbcrlf & sResults
	Else
		FTPUpload = "Error: Unknown."
		MsgBox "Hata : " & vbcrlf & sResults
	End If

	Set oFTPScriptFSO = Nothing
	Set oFTPScriptShell = Nothing
End Function

Sub Makro1()

	Dim May_container
	Set May_container = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	May_container.Addfield2 "DOSYA","CHAR",255,0,"L","F2=DO_SYSBRW_FILENAME()",""
	satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
	May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	
End Sub

Dim mesaj_array
Redim mesaj_array(20)

SUB Getir()

	Set TABLO_1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")

	if trim(TABLO_1.DOSYA(1)) <> "" then

		Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

		Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(TABLO_1.DOSYA(1), False, True)

		Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	else 
	
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

		exit sub

	end if
	
	Dim k
	
	k = 1

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("PERSM22", "")
	Set P_PERSM22 = Doc1.GetTableObject("PERSM22")
	Set P_PERSM22_B = Doc1.GetTableObject("PERSM22_B")

	for j = 2 to Sh1.UsedRange.Rows.Count
		if	 trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,1)) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(UCase(trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,1))))
			P_PERSM22.MESLEKADI = 	trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,2))
			P_PERSM22.MESLEKTANIMI = trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,3))
			P_PERSM22_B.GOREVISLEMLER = trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,4))

			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		end if
	Next

	Wb1.Close

	Excel.Quit

	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FOSN-4

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : OTOSP1
Açýklama : Sipariþleri günlüðe parçala
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call AylikSiparisleriHaftaligaParcala()
	OTOSP1L.SafePack()
	i = 0
	Do
		i = i + 1
		if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRowCount()
		if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) = "H" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "AUF9A" and _
													trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "CVR1B" Then
			IlkPazartesi = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i)
			'KalanGunSayisi = DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi(IlkPazartesi, OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i))
			KalanGunSayisi = 6
			GunlukMiktar = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i), KalanGunSayisi))
			DagitilacakMiktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
			ret = OTOSP1L.ExportRowToXML(i)
			ret = Replace(ret, "<?xml version=""1.0""?>", "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""?>")
			Attrib = OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i)
			For j = 1 to KalanGunSayisi
				if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
				Miktar = GunlukMiktar
				if Kalan < Miktar Then Miktar = Kalan
				Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
				if j > 1 Then
					i = i + 1
					if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount Then
						OTOSP1L.AddRow
					Else
						OTOSP1L.InsertRow(i)
					End if
				End if
				OTOSP1L.SetCurrentRow(i)
				TrNum = OTOSP1L.TRNUM
				if j > 1 Then OTOSP1L.ImportRowFromXML i, ret
				OTOSP1L.TRNUM = TrNum
				OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "G"
				OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(IlkPazartesi, j-1)
				OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH
			Next
		End if
	Loop
End Sub
Function GetNextWeekAndDay(Tarih, Hafta, Gun)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	if d7Date.GetDOW() = Gun and Hafta > 0 Then Hafta = Hafta - 1
	d7Date.Increment Hafta * 7
	HaftaGun = d7Date.GetDOW()
	d7Date.Increment Gun - HaftaGun
	GetNextWeekAndDay = d7Date.GetRevDate
End Function
Function DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi(Tarih, Attrib)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	if Attrib = "A" Then
		d7Date.SetDate Tarih
		d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
		DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi = Doc.CDateToInt(d7Date.GetRevDate) - Doc.CDateToInt(Tarih) + 1
	Else
		DonemSonunaKadarGunSayisi = 7
	End if
End Function
Sub AylikSiparisleriHaftaligaParcala()
	if Doc.IsBlank(OTOSP1L) Then Set OTOSP1L = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1L")
	OTOSP1L.SafePack()
	i = 0
	Do
		i = i + 1
		if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & OTOSP1L.GetRowCount()
		if OTOSP1L.ATTRIB(i) = "A" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "AUF9A" and trim(OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i)) <> "CVR1B" Then
			IlkPazartesi = ""
			QStr = "KOD='" & OTOSP1L.KOD(i) & "'&SEVK_EDILEN_FB='" & OTOSP1L.SEVK_EDILEN_FB(i) & "'&ATTRIB<>'A'"
			Set OSP1L = Doc.Select2("sdrgw4gs", "OTOSP1L", QStr, "<RTESTARIH", -1)
			if OSP1L.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				IlkPazartesi = GetNextWeekAndDay(Doc.StrDate_Increment(OSP1L.RTESTARIH(1), 1), 1, 1)
			End if
			AyinIlkPazartesiGunu = GetNextWeekAndDay(OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH(i), 1, 1)
			if AyinIlkPazartesiGunu > IlkPazartesi Then IlkPazartesi = AyinIlkPazartesiGunu

			KalanHaftaSayisi = AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi(IlkPazartesi)
			HaftalikMiktar = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i), KalanHaftaSayisi))
			DagitilacakMiktar = OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
			ret = OTOSP1L.ExportRowToXML(i)
			ret = Replace(ret, "<?xml version=""1.0""?>", "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""?>")
			For j = 1 to KalanHaftaSayisi			
				if Kalan <= 0 Then Exit For
				Miktar = HaftalikMiktar
				if Kalan < Miktar Then Miktar = Kalan
				Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
				if j > 1 Then
					i = i + 1
					if i > OTOSP1L.GetRowCount Then
						OTOSP1L.AddRow
					Else
						OTOSP1L.InsertRow(i)
					End if
				End if
				OTOSP1L.SetCurrentRow(i)
				TrNum = OTOSP1L.TRNUM
				if j > 1 Then OTOSP1L.ImportRowFromXML i, ret
				OTOSP1L.TRNUM = TrNum
				OTOSP1L.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
				OTOSP1L.ATTRIB = "H"
				OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(IlkPazartesi, (j-1) * 7)
				OTOSP1L.REV_RTESTARIH = OTOSP1L.RTESTARIH
			Next
		End if
	Loop
End Sub
Function KopyalanacakMi(AlanAdi)
	KopyalanacakMi = True
	if AlanAdi = "EVRAKNO" or AlanAdi = "TRNUM" or AlanAdi = "SRNUM" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00_") > 0 _
			or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_CARI00_") > 0 Then
		KopyalanacakMi = False
	End if
End Function
Function AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi(Tarih)
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	KalanGun = Doc.CDateToInt(d7Date.GetRevDate) - Doc.CDateToInt(Tarih) + 1
	AySonunaKadarHaftaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(KalanGun / 7)
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KASA-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK26
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	STOK26T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to STOK26T.GetRowCount()
		if Left(STOK26T.KOD(i), 1) = "5" Then
			STOK26T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			STOK26T.KOD(i) = ""
			STOK26T.LOTNUMBER(i) = ""
			STOK26T.SERINO(i) = ""
			STOK26T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = 0
			STOK26T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	STOK26T.SafePack()

	Set S26T = Doc.Select2("XXX_STOK26T_KASA", "STOK26T", "", "", -1)
	S26T.GroupBy("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S26T.GetRowCount()
		KasaKodu = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_1", S26T.KOD(i), "")
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_1", S26T.KOD(i), "")
		KMiktar=Doc.Div(S26T.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar)
		S26T.KOD(i) = KasaKodu
		S26T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = KMiktar
	Next
	S26T.GroupBy("KOD")

	For i = 1 to S26T.GetRowCount()
		STOK26T.AddRow()
		satirNo = STOK26T.GetRowCount()
		STOK26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK26T.KOD(satirNo) = S26T.KOD(i)
		STOK26T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S26T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
End Sub

Kod : 
Glo_path="C:\ISA\HAZÝRAN.XLS"
Glo_satirsayisi = 300
Sub Makro1()

	colNormalCalisma = 4
	colHaftaTatili = 7
	colGenelTatil = 8
	colYillikIzin = 13
	colUcretliIzin = 14
	colDogumIzni = 18
	colOlumIzni = 19
	colEvlenmeIzni = 20
	colDoktorIzni = 23
	colSendikaIzni = 24
	colUcretsizIzin = 15
	colIstirahat = 17
	colMesai50 = 9
	colMesai100 = 12
	colNormalCalismasiGece = 5
	colMesai50Gece = 10
	colMesai100Gece = 11


	dim strFpath,intSatirSayisi
	Fpath = Glo_path
	strFpath = inputbox("Excel dosyasýnýn yolunu giriniz","Puantaj dosyasýnýn yolu",Glo_path)
	if trim(strFpath)="" Then 
		msgbox("Boþ dosya adý olamaz.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if
	intSatirSayisi = inputbox("Excel dosyasýndan okunacak satýr sayýsý","Excel'deki satýr sayýsý",Glo_satirsayisi)
	if not isNumeric(intSatirSayisi) Then 
		msgbox("Boþ ya da yanlýþ satýr bildirimi.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if
	dim objExcel
	Set objExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	dim objWorkbooks
	Set objWorkbooks = objExcel.Workbooks
	objWorkbooks.Open(strFpath)
	
	dim objSheet1
	Set objSheet1 = objWorkbooks(1).Worksheets(1)
	dim c,strPersNo
	dim dblNormalCalisma,dblHaftatatili,dblGenelTatil,dblYillikizin,dblucretliizin,dbldogum
	dim dblOlum,dblEvlenme,dblDoktor,dblSendika
	dim dblIstirahat,dblucretsiz,dbl100mesai,dbl50mesai

	if err.number<>0 Then 
		objWorkbooks(1).Close
		objExcel.Quit
		Set objExcel=Nothing
		Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
		Set objSheet1=Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End if	

	For c=1 to BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Puantaj Bilgileri Aktarýlýyor : " & c & " / " & BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		'strPersNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		SicilNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		strPersNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("PERSM0","EHLIYETNO",Sicilno,"")
		BRDR1PBGIRIS.SetCurrentRow(c)
		if TRUE Then
			Sutun=1
			For ik=2 to Glo_satirsayisi
				if Trim(objSheet1.Cells(ik,sutun))=trim(strPersNo) Then
					satir = ik
					Exit For
				End if
			Next

			strPersNoExcel = objSheet1.Cells(satir,sutun).Value
			i = satir
	
			if trim(strPersNoExcel) <> "" Then
				if Trim(strPersNoExcel)=Trim(strPersNo) Then

					'NORMAL ÇALIÞMA
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalisma).Value) Then
						dblNormalCalisma=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalisma).Value)
					Else
						dblNormalCalisma=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'HAFTA TATÝLÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colHaftaTatili).Value) Then
						dblHaftatatili=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colHaftaTatili).Value)
					Else
						dblhaftatatili=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'GENEL TATÝL
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colGenelTatil).Value) Then
						dblGenelTatil=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colGenelTatil).Value)
					Else
						dblGenelTatil=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'YILLIK IZIN
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colYillikIzin).Value) Then
						dblYillikizin=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colYillikIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblYillikizin=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'ÜCRETLÝ ÝZÝN 
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretliIzin).Value) Then
						dblucretliizin=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretliIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblucretliizin=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'DOÐUM ÝZNÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDogumIzni).Value) Then
						dblDogum=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDogumIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblDogum=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'ÖLÜM ÝZNÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colOlumIzni).Value) Then
						dblOlum=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colOlumIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblOlum=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'EVLENME ÝZNÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colEvlenmeIzni).Value) Then
						dblEvlenme=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colEvlenmeIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblEvlenme=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'DOKTOR ÝZNÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDoktorIzni).Value) Then
						dblDoktor=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDoktorIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblDoktor=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'SENDÝKA ÝZNÝ
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colSendikaIzni).Value) Then
						dblSendika=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colSendikaIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblSendika=cdbl(0)
					End if

 					'ÜCRETSÝZ ÝZÝN
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretsizIzin).Value) Then
						dblucretsiz=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretsizIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblucretsiz=cdbl(0)
					End if
 					'ÝSTÝRAHAT
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colIstirahat).Value) Then
						dblIstirahat=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colIstirahat).Value)
					Else
						dblIstirahat=cdbl(0)
					End if

					'%50 MESAI (SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50).Value) Then
						dbl50mesai=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50).Value)
					Else
						dbl50mesai=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'%100 MESAI(SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100).Value) Then 
						dbl100mesai=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100).Value)
					Else
						dbl100mesai=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Normal Çalýþma
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalismasiGece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceCalisma=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalismasiGece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceCalisma=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Mesai %50
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50Gece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceMesai50=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50Gece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceMesai50=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Mesai %100
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100Gece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceMesai100=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100Gece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceMesai100=cdbl(0)
					End if

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS01(c) = dblNormalCalisma
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS02(c) = dblHaftatatili
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS03(c) = dblGenelTatil
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS04(c) = dblYillikizin
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS05(c) = dblucretliizin-dblDoktor
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS06(c) = dbldogum
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS07(c) = dblOlum
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS08(c) = dblEvlenme
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS09(c) = dblDoktor
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS10(c) = dblSendika

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIS01(c) = dblIstirahat
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIS02(c) = dblucretsiz

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E01(c) = dbl50mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E02(c) = dbl100mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E03(c) = dblGeceCalisma
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E04(c) = dblGeceMesai50
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E05(c) = dblGeceMesai100


					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = Doc.div(dblNormalCalisma,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c) = Doc.div(dblHaftatatili,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c) = Doc.div(dblGenelTatil,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) = Doc.div(dblYillikizin,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c) = Doc.div(dblucretliizin,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c) = Doc.div(dbldogum,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) = Doc.div(dblOlum,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c) = Doc.div(dblEvlenme,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c) = Doc.div(dblDoktor,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c) = Doc.div(dblSendika,7.5)

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS01(c) = Doc.div(dblIstirahat,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS02(c) = Doc.div(dblUcretsiz,7.5)
		
					'Günleri tam deðer yap
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c))
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c))		

		
					SSKToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) _ 
					+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) _
					+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS02(c)
						
					GirilmesiGirenSSK = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_GEREKEN_SSKPRIMGUNU(c) 

					if SSKToplami > GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark = SSKToplami - GirilmesiGirenSSK
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)-Fark
					End if
					if SSKToplami < GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark1 = GirilmesiGirenSSK - SSKToplami
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)+Fark1
					End if
				

				End if
			End if
'			if err.number<>0 Then 
			if False Then 
				MSGBOX "Hata " & Err.Number & " " & Error()
				objWorkbooks(1).Close
				objExcel.Quit
				Exit Sub
				Set objExcel=Nothing
				Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
				Set objSheet1=Nothing
			End if	
		End if
		if False Then 
			objWorkbooks(1).Close
			objExcel.Quit
			Set objExcel=Nothing
			Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
			Set objSheet1=Nothing
			Exit Sub
		End if	
	Next
	
	objWorkbooks(1).Close
	objExcel.Quit

	Set objExcel=Nothing
	Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
	Set objSheet1=Nothing
	msgbox "Dosya aktarma iþlemi bitti."
End Sub

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TNT3
Hangi Tablo için : TNT00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TNT yükleme formunu sipariþine aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(TNT00E.TESLIM) = "" or trim(TNT00E.CH_KODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve teslim yerini girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set f = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Hata = ""
	
	For i = 1 to TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "TNT Yükleme Formu Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		if TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i) > 0 Then
			ST40.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = TNT00T.KOD(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = TNT00E.TESLIM
			Gk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_4", TNT00T.KOD(i), "")
			if InStr(1, TNT00E.TESLIM, "D1D4F") > 0 and trim(Gk4) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox TNT00T.KOD(i) & " için Grup Kodu-4 girilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
				Hata = "x"
			End if
			ST40.NOTES(satirNo) = Gk4
		end if
	Next
	
	if trim(Hata) <> "" Then Exit Sub
	
	
	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")
	ST40.Sort("NOTES+REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")
	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	
	EvrakSiraNo = -1
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		Gk4 = ST40.NOTES(i)
		if Gk4 <> EskiGk4 Then
			if trim(ST40E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
				if nret = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "", MB_OK
					Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo, EvrakSiraNo)
				End if
			End if
			EvrakSiraNo = EvrakSiraNo + 1
			'ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
			Call YeniSipVeyaYukle(Doc1, EvrakSiraNo)
			EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
			if nret <> 0 then

				k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
			
			ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
			ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
			ST40E.CHSIPNO = TNT00E.EVRAKNO
			ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""
			
			ST40T.Empty
		End if
		
		satirNo = ST40T.AddRow
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Satýþ sipariþine aktarýlýyor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()
		
		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.NOTES(satirNo) = ST40.NOTES(i)
		EskiGk4 = Gk4
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
	Else
		nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo, EvrakSiraNo)
		Doc.d7MsgBox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipariþ yaratýldý", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo, EvrakSiraNo)
	Dim T_TNT00E
	Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")
	Dim nret
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)
	Dim T_U1
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")
	if EvrakSiraNo = 0 Then
		AlanAdi = "SIPARISNO"
	Else
		AlanAdi = "SIPARISNO_" & EvrakSiraNo
	End if
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(AlanAdi,-1,SiparisNo)
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"
End Sub
Sub YeniSipVeyaYukle(Doc1, EvrakSiraNo)
	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("TNT00E", TNT00E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO", EvrakSiraNo)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set ddee3 = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	STOK63T.SafePack()
	Set S63T = Doc.Select2("de3df4", "STOK63T", "KOD<'5'", "", -1)
	S63T.GroupBy2("KOD")
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
	Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S46E.CH_KODU = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	S46E.FASON10 = "1"

	For i = 1 to S63T.GetRowCount()
		KodMiktar = NeredeKullaniliyor(S63T.KOD(i), S63T.SF_MIKTAR(i))
		if KodMiktar(0) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox S63T.KOD(i) & " nerede kullanýldýðý bulunamadýðý içim satýnalma sipariþine eklenmedi", "", MB_OK
		Else
			satirNo = S46T.AddRow()
			S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S46T.KOD = KodMiktar(0)
			S46T.SF_MIKTAR = KodMiktar(1)
			S46T.RTESTARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(STOK60E.TARIH, 2)
		End if
	Next

	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if nret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox S46E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu satýnalma sipariþi yaratýldý", "Ýþlem tamamlandý", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function NeredeKullaniliyor(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Dim BomArray(1)
	BomArray(0) = ""
	BomArray(1) = 0
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("de3df55", "BOMU01T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		BomArray(0) = B01T.BOMREC_CODE
		BomArray(1) = Doc.Div(Miktar * B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0, B01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR)
	End if
	NeredeKullaniliyor = BomArray
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FIYAT1
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK00
Açýklama : Envanter maliyetini hesaplayan makro (silmeyin) - Satýnalmalarda son 3 aylýk ortalama
Kod : 
Dim S48, Doc_Old, TezgahSaatUcreti
Sub Makro1()
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK00" Then
		BitisTarihi = Doc.BuGun
	Else
		BitisTarihi = "2014/04/30"
	End if
	BaslangicTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BitisTarihi, -30)

	Set SatinalmaTable = GetSatinalmaTable(BaslangicTarihi, BitisTarihi)
	'MsgBox GetFiyat("011 0000-0000-D1", "", BitisTarihi, SatinalmaTable)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK00" Then
		StokKodu = STOK00.KOD
		Miktar = Doc.Cdbl(InputBox("Miktar?"))
	Else
'******		
'Ctrl+Shift+F12 -> Test Modu
		StokKodu = "2FA BK21-V451A14-BH"
		Miktar = 6600
'***********
	End if
	Set Tbl1 = GetDetayTLFiyat(StokKodu, "", Miktar, BaslangicTarihi, BitisTarihi, SatinalmaTable)

	Doc.ShowTableDynForm2 Tbl1.GetAppName, "KOD,BOMREC_KAYNAK0,ADAM_SAAT,SAC_MIKTAR,SAC_TL,SACKESIM_TL,DIGERMLZ_MIKTAR,DIGERMLZ_TL,FASON_TL,ISCILIK_TL,GIDER_TL,KALIP_TL,HURDA_TL"
End Sub
Function GetSatinalmaTable(BaslangicTarihi, BitisTarihi)
	OncekiFirma = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")

	Set d = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKTYPE, KOD, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR, SUM(TL_TUTAR) TL_TUTAR, Sum(TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM ("
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT EVRAKTYPE, KOD, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR, SUM(TL_TUTAR) TL_TUTAR, Sum(TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK10A"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE ((EVRAKTYPE='SI' and TL_TUTAR>0) or (EVRAKTYPE='FG' AND TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR>0))"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH>='" & BaslangicTarihi & "' AND TARIH<='" & BitisTarihi & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	GROUP BY EVRAKTYPE, KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " 	UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT EVRAKTYPE, KOD, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR, SUM(TL_TUTAR) TL_TUTAR, Sum(TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM " & trim(OncekiFirma) & ".STOK10A"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE ((EVRAKTYPE='SI' and TL_TUTAR>0) or (EVRAKTYPE='FG' AND TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR>0))"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH>='" & BaslangicTarihi & "' AND TARIH<='" & BitisTarihi & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	GROUP BY EVRAKTYPE, KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " )"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY EVRAKTYPE, KOD"		
	Set GetSatinalmaTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery("xxx_SQL_S10A", QStr)
End Function

Function GetFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)
	TezgahSaatUcreti = 0.35
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXXX__1=STOK48T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S48 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXXX__1")
	
	GetFiyat = GetTLFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)

	S48.GroupBy2("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S48.GetRowCount()
		MsgStr = MsgStr & S48.KOD(i) & " " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S48.KOD(i), "") & " fiyat bulunamadý." & vbcrlf
	Next
	if trim(MsgStr) <> "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýnalma Fiyat Listelerinde fiyatý bulunamayan kodlar " & vbcrlf & MsgStr, "", MB_OK
	End if
End Function

Function GetTLFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)
	if Left(StokKodu, 1) = "0" Then
		GetTLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)
		Exit Function
	End if
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu, 1, 1, "", FOKodu, "", "", "", "", "", "") 
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		GetTLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)
		Exit Function
	End if
	TLFiyat = 0
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 0 Then
				TLFiyat = TLFiyat + GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Tarih, Tbl_S10A) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
			Else
				TLFiyat = TLFiyat + GetFasonFiyat(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Tbl_S10A) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
			End if
		Else
			if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 2) <> "FS" Then
				TLFiyat = TLFiyat + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) * TezgahSaatUcreti
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetTLFiyat = TLFiyat
End Function

Function GetDetayTLFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Miktar, BTarih, ETarih, Tbl_S10A)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXXX__1=STOK48T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S48 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXXX__1")

	Set Tbl1 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("FIYAT_TBL001=STACK", "TMPCOPY")
	Tbl1.AddField2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAK0","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "ADAM_SAAT","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "SAC_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "SAC_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "SACKESIM_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "DIGERMLZ_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",10,3,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "DIGERMLZ_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "FASON_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "ISCILIK_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "GIDER_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "KALIP_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.AddField2 "HURDA_TL","DOUBLE",10,2,"R","",""
	Tbl1.Empty
	Set GetDetayTLFiyat = Tbl1

	if Left(StokKodu, 1) = "0" Then
		TLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, ETarih, Tbl_S10A)
		satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
		Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
		if Left(StokKodu, 3) = "011" Then
			Tbl1.SAC_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			Tbl1.SAC_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat * Miktar
		Else
			Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat * Miktar
		End if
		Exit Function
	End if

	OncekYil = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")
	QStr = "SELECT TOP_BOMREC_CODE, BOMREC_INPUTTYPE, BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE, BOMREC_KAYNAK0, (BOMREC_KAYNAK0+BOMREC_KAYNAK1) KAYNAKMIKTART, IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU,"
	QStr = QStr & " (CASE WHEN (OPERATORSAYISI1+OPERATORSAYISI2+OPERATORSAYISI3) = 0 THEN 1 "
	QStr = QStr & " 	ELSE OPERATORSAYISI1+OPERATORSAYISI2+OPERATORSAYISI3 END) * BOMREC_KAYNAK0 ADAM_SAAT"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM TABLE(CALC_BOM_PKG.CALC_BOM('" & StokKodu & "'," & Miktar & "))"
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.RunSQLQuery("BOM_OP_LIST", QStr)

'	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu, 1, Miktar, "", FOKodu, "", "", "", "", "", "") 
'	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		TLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, ETarih, Tbl_S10A)
		satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
		Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
		if Left(StokKodu, 3) = "011" Then
			Tbl1.SAC_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			Tbl1.SAC_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat * Miktar
		Else
			Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Miktar
			Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat * Miktar
		End if

		Exit Function
	End if
	TLFiyat = 0

	FasonFiyati = GetFasonFiyat(StokKodu, Tbl_S10A) * Miktar
	if FasonFiyati > 0 Then
		satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
		Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu
		Tbl1.FASON_TL(satirNo) = FasonFiyati
	End if

	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 0 Then
				satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
				Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)

				TLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ETarih, Tbl_S10A) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)

				if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 3) = "011" Then
					Tbl1.SAC_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
					Tbl1.SAC_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat

					QStr = "SELECT Sum(TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR) / Sum(SF_STOK_MIKTAR) FIYAT"
					QStr = QStr & " FROM ("
					QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR, SF_STOK_MIKTAR"
					QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK74T"
					QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE KOD='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TL_FIYAT>0 AND TL_FIYAT<0.8"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH>='" & Doc.StrDate_Increment(ETarih, -30) & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH<='" & ETarih & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND SF_STOK_MIKTAR>0"
					QStr = QStr & " 	UNION ALL"
					QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR, SF_STOK_MIKTAR"
					QStr = QStr & " 	FROM " & (OncekYil) & ".STOK74T"
					QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE KOD='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TL_FIYAT>0 AND TL_FIYAT<0.8"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH>='" & Doc.StrDate_Increment(ETarih, -30) & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH<='" & ETarih & "'"
					QStr = QStr & " 	AND SF_STOK_MIKTAR>0"
					QStr = QStr & " )"
					QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY 1"
					Set S74T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfasdfa", QStr)
					if S74T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
						Tbl1.SACKESIM_TL(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i) * S74T.FIYAT(1)
					End if
				Else
					Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
					Tbl1.DIGERMLZ_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat
				End if
			Else
				FasonFiyati = GetFasonFiyat(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Tbl_S10A) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
				if FasonFiyati > 0 Then
					satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
					Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
					Tbl1.FASON_TL(satirNo) = FasonFiyati
				End if
			End if
		Elseif Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "Y" Then
			QStr = "SELECT * FROM ("
			QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR / SF_MIKTAR TL_FIYAT"
			QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK70T"
			QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE KOD='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"
			QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH<='" & ETarih & "'"
			QStr = QStr & " 	ORDER BY TARIH DESC"
			QStr = QStr & " ) WHERE ROWNUM=1"
			Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdfasg", QStr)
			satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
			Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			if S70T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				Tbl1.HURDA_TL(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i) * S70T.TL_FIYAT(1)
			End if
		Elseif Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "I" and Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 5) <> "FASON" Then
			satirNo = Tbl1.AddRow
			Tbl1.KOD(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			Tbl1.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			Tbl1.ADAM_SAAT(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.ADAM_SAAT(i)

			QStr = "SELECT UNSUR, SUM(TUTAR/MIKTAR) TUTAR, PARABIRIMI"
			QStr = QStr & " FROM STDMI0T"
			QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"			
			QStr = QStr & " AND STDMALTYPE='FM'"
			QStr = QStr & " AND VALIDAFTERTARIH=("
			QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT MAX(VALIDAFTERTARIH) "
			QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STDMI0T"
			QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE KOD='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"			
			QStr = QStr & " 	AND STDMALTYPE='FM'"
			QStr = QStr & " )"
			QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY UNSUR, PARABIRIMI"
			Set I0T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfasdfa", QStr)
			For j = 1 to I0T.GetRowCount()
				TLFiyat = Tbmrsys.ADAM_SAAT(i) * I0T.TUTAR(j)
				if trim(I0T.UNSUR(j)) = "IM" Then
					Tbl1.ISCILIK_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat
				Elseif trim(I0T.UNSUR(j)) = "GG" Then
					Tbl1.GIDER_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat
				Elseif trim(I0T.UNSUR(j)) = "KB" Then
					Tbl1.KALIP_TL(satirNo) = TLFiyat
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
	Set GetDetayTLFiyat = Tbl1
End Function

Function GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih, Tbl_S10A)
	GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = 0

	Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
	Set S10A = Doc.Select2("huh88hh", Tbl_S10A.GetAppName, "EVRAKTYPE='SI'&KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	If S10A.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = Doc.Div(S10A.TL_TUTAR(1) + S10A.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S10A.STOK_MIKTAR(1))	
		Exit Function
	End If

	Set S48T = Doc.Select1("mddrrdddd", "STOK48T", "KULLANIM_YERI='2'&KOD='"+StokKodu+"'", "", -1)
	if S48T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Kur = Doc.GetKurN(S48T.PRICEUNIT(1), Doc.DefaultPB, Tarih, "", -1)
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
		BirimDonusumu = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(StokKodu, Birim, S48T.SF_UNIT(1), d7c_ErrMsg_No)
		GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = S48T.PRICE(1) * Kur * BirimDonusumu
		if GetSatinalmaTLFiyati > 0 Then Exit Function
	End if
	if GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = 0 Then
		GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.FIYAT", "GetFiyat", StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih)
	End if
	if GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = 0 Then
		ss = S48.AddRow
		S48.KOD(ss) = StokKodu
	End if
End Function
Function GetFasonFiyat(StokKodu, Tbl_S10A)
	GetFasonFiyat = 0
	Set S68T = Doc.Select2("ssw1weeqqse", Tbl_S10A.GetAppName, "EVRAKTYPE='FG'&KOD='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'", "", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetFasonFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.STOK_MIKTAR(1))
		Exit Function
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT * FROM ("
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR/STOK_MIKTAR FIYAT"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK10A"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE EVRAKTYPE='FG'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND KOD='" & StokKodu & "'" 
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR>0"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND STOK_MIKTAR>0"
	QStr = QStr & " 	ORDER BY TARIH DESC"
	QStr = QStr & " ) WHERE ROWNUM=1"
	Set S68T2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sswweeqqxx", QStr)
if trim(StokKodu) = "011 0120-7114-01" Then MsgBox S68T2.GetRowCount()

	if S68T2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetFasonFiyat = S68T2.FIYAT
	End if
End Function

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK46T_LISTESI2.GetRowCount()
		if STOK46T_LISTESI2.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiEvrakNo Then
			if trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.Load_Voucher STOK46T_LISTESI2.EVRAKNO(i)
		End if
		EskiEvrakNo = STOK46T_LISTESI2.EVRAKNO(i)
		Set S46Tx = Doc.Select2("fsdf4fsdg", "STOK46T", "ARTNO='" & STOK46T_LISTESI2.ARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S46Tx.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			satirNo = Doc.CDbl(S46Tx.SRNUM(i))
			if S46Tx.SF_NETKAPANANMIK > 0 Then
				STOK46T.ACIKKAPALI(satirNo) = "K"
			Else
				STOK46T.KOD(satirNo) = ""
				STOK46T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ""
				STOK46T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ""
				STOK46T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ""
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SA-SIP
Hangi Tablo için : STOKRC2
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Sipariþlerini Aç
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	KesinSiparisTarihi = InputBox("Hangi Tarihe Kadar Olanlar Kesin Sipariþe Dönüþecek", "", Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.BuGun, 1)))
	if trim(KesinSiparisTarihi) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S47T = Doc.Select2("dfsaedf23fsts", "STOK47T", "TEDARIKCI_CH_T='" & Space(16) & "'", "", -1)
	if S47T.GetRowCount() = STOK47T.GetRowCount() Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Tedarikçi ve Son Satýnalma Bilgileri butonuna basýlmalýdýr", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	Elseif S47T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Tüm satýrlarda tedarikçi kodu seçilmiþ olmalýdýr", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
	Set S47T = Doc.Select2("dfsaedf23fsts", "STOK47T", "", "", -1)
	S47T.GroupBy("TEDARIKCI_CH_T")
	For i = 1 to S47T.GetRowCount()
		Call SatinalmaSiparisiniYarat(Doc1, S47T.TEDARIKCI_CH_T(i), KesinSiparisTarihi)
	Next
End Sub
Sub SatinalmaSiparisiniYarat(Doc1, CHKodu, KesinSiparisTarihi)
	KesinTeslimTarihi = Right(KesinSiparisTarihi, 4) & "/" & Mid(KesinSiparisTarihi,4 ,2) & "/" & Left(KesinSiparisTarihi, 2)
	Set S47T = Doc.Select2G("fsfe4fdsdh", "STOK47T", "TEDARIKCI_CH_T='" & CHKodu & "'", "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH+KOD", "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH+KOD", -1)

	Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")	
	Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")	

	nret = Doc1.New_Voucher
	if nret <> 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yeni Evrak Yaratýlamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	S46E.CH_KODU = CHKodu

	For i = 1 to S47T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþ Yaratýlýyor : " & trim(CHKodu) & "-" & i & " / " & S47T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S46T.AddRow()
		S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S46T.KOD = S47T.KOD(i)
		S46T.SF_MIKTAR = S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S46T.RTESTARIH = S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(i)
		if S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(i) <= KesinTeslimTarihi Then
			S46T.STATUS = "K"
			S46T.SPSTATUSU = "K"
			S46T.MRPICINSABITLEME10 = "1"
		Else
			S46T.STATUS = "T"
			S46T.SPSTATUSU = "T"
			S46T.MRPICINSABITLEME10 = "0"
			S46T.SIRALIKAPATMADISI = "1"
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DSTL_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TEDARIK2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Sipariþlerini tedarikçilere göre böl, mail gönder.
Kod : 
Public SP,stokkodu,firma
Sub Makro1()
	
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","STOK46T")


	baslangicsiparis = InputBox("Baþlangýç Satýþ Sipariþinin Evrak Numarasýný Giriniz","Baþlangýç Satýþ Sipariþi ","")
	bitissiparis = InputBox("Bitiþ Satýþ Sipariþinin Evrak Numarasýný Giriniz","Bitiþ Satýþ Sipariþi ","")

	doc.cint(baslangicsiparis)	
	doc.cint(bitissiparis)

	if baslangicsiparis > bitissiparis then
		k=doc.d7msgbox("Yanlýþ evrak aralýðý verdiniz, Lütfen bitiþ Evrak Numarasý Baþlangýç Evrak Numarasýndan Büyük olmalý","",MB_ICONWARNING)
		Exit sub
	end if

	QStr = "EVRAKNO>='"& Doc.RightJustify(trim(baslangicsiparis), 10) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&EVRAKNO<='"& Doc.RightJustify(trim(bitissiparis ), 10) & "'"
	Set SP = Doc.Select1("1ABC","STOK46T",QStr,"<EVRAKNO",-1)

		for i = 1 to SP.GetRecCount()
			Firma = SP.CH_KODU(i)
			Firmaadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Firma, "")
			SP.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = SP.EVRAKNO(i)
		Next


	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	SP.Sort("CH_KODU")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	EskiFirma = ""

	TsatirNo = 0

	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excele Yazýlýyor : " & i & " / " & SP.GetRowCount()
		stokkodu = SP.KOD(i)
		Firma = SP.CH_KODU(i)
		Firmaadi = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Firma, ""))

		if trim(Firma) <> "" Then
			TsatirNo = TsatirNo + 1
			DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & LEFT(trim(firmaadi),10) & ".xls"
			if EskiFirma <> Firma Then
				if TsatirNo <> 1 Then
					Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
					Wb.Save
					xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
					Wb.Close
					Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 10), xlsDosya)
				End if			
				Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
				Wb.SaveAs DosyaAdi
				Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
				satirNo = 1
				Sutun = 0
				for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
					AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
					if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or AlanAdi ="SF_MIKTAR" or AlanAdi ="SF_SF_UNIT" or AlanAdi ="SF_VRI_VR_R3" or AlanAdi ="REV_RTESTARIH" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
						Sutun = Sutun + 1
						Baslik = Grid(j,2)
						if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Baslik
							if AlanAdi = "SF_VRI_VR_R3" Then
								Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "Sipariþ No"
							Else
								Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Baslik
							End if
					End if
				Next
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			End if
			Sutun = 0
			for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
				AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
					if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or AlanAdi ="SF_MIKTAR" or AlanAdi ="SF_SF_UNIT" or AlanAdi ="SF_VRI_VR_R3" or AlanAdi ="REV_RTESTARIH" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
					Sutun = Sutun + 1
					Baslik = Grid(j,2)
					if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For

					if AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", SP.KOD(i), "")
					Else
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = SP.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
					End if
				End if
			Next
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			EskiFirma = Firma
		End if
	Next

	if TsatirNo > 0 Then
		Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
		Wb.Save
		xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
		Wb.Close
		Excel.Quit
		Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 10), xlsDosya)
	End if
End Sub
'Sub MailGonder(GrupKodu9, xlsDosya)
Sub MailGonder(firma, xlsDosya)
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(1,"h",-1,-1,"")
	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.SetMailSubject(Firmaadi & " Program - " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)

	Set r = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
'	mailAdresi = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK9", "AD", GrupKodu9, -1)
	mailAdresi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "EMAIL", firma, "")

	if trim(mailAdresi) = "" Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Grup Kodu-9 : " & stokkodu & ", Stok Kodlu ürünün Grup Kodu açýklamasýna mail adresi girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
		Doc.d7MsgBox "E-Posta : " & firma & ", Cari Hesap Kodlu firmanýn " & "'" & "e-Mail"& "'" &" alanýna mail adresi girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.ClearMailReceivers()

	res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(trim(mailAdresi),1)
	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(xlsDosya,0)
	res = pe1.Maildoc()
	res = pe1.Close()
End Sub
Sub Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1), Sh1.Cells(1,256)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1), Sh1.Cells(1,2)).Select
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit
End Sub
